in the move (verified: this file's entry count and content bytes were diffed against
the removed roadmap note content programmatically before commit).

Found and fixed: `art-styler.vue`'s in-progress "Applying X via Kontext…" banner bound directly to the live `selectedStyle` ref, but nothing in the component blocks style/source reselection while a generation is in flight (deliberate, per the component's own `generationToken` comment — the result/error handlers already guard against a stale request clobbering a newer selection). A user who reselected a different style mid-generation saw the banner's name change to the new style while the actual in-flight request — and its eventual, correctly-attributed result — was still for the original style, a visible contradiction. Fixed by snapshotting the generating style's label into a new `generatingStyleLabel` ref at the top of `runStyleTransfer` (alongside the existing `const style = selectedStyle.value` capture used for the request itself) and binding the banner to that instead of the live ref. Verified: `npx eslint`/`npx prettier --check` on the changed file both clean, full-project `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) exit 0. Merged kind_robots PR #1546, all CI checks green. `python scripts/validate_roadmaps.py` and `python scripts/audit_roadmaps.py` both clean before and after. No image generation jobs, production mutation, publishing, spend, secrets, or DNS changes occurred. Next preferred lane is roadmap accuracy (lane 2), per the 1->2->3->4 rotation. Rearming to `ready`.

RAN 2026-08-05T23:36:15Z (conductor scheduled agent run, session 2026-08-05T232803Z-aa-t010-lane1): option (a), front-end polish (lane 1), per `continuous_improvement.next_lane: 1` (the field, not the note's stale "Next lane: 2" text left by the PR #1506 backfill below — the field never actually advanced past lane 4 for that one, so this cycle is the real lane-1 pass). Read all 5 Academy components end to end looking for a genuine, contained gap not already covered by the extensive prior lane-1 history in this file. Found one in `academy-manager.vue`: the tab watcher configures a shared `uploadStore` singleton target for Style Lab's ArtImage upload on entering that tab, but only ever cleared it on the whole component's `onUnmounted` — switching to Timeline/Styles/Remix within Academy left the shared target pointed at Style Lab's upload for as long as Academy stayed mounted, so any other consumer of that same shared target elsewhere on the page (a global upload trigger) would keep offering "Upload image" for a tab the user already left. Fixed by clearing the target in the same `watch(activeTab, ...)` callback whenever the tab is anything other than `stylelab`, mirroring the existing unmount-time clear. Verified: `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) exit 0; `npx prettier --check` clean after one `--write` pass; `npm run test:academy-style-detail-callers` (sanity, same component family) passes. kind_robots PR #1508 (branch `claude/academy-upload-target-cleanup-lane1`), all 15 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers x2, layout-contract, facet-catalog, 5 narrative/Storybook/Taskmaster contracts, GitGuardian, Vercel Preview Comments) plus the Vercel preview itself reaching `READY` — merged squash `c1821d5`. Also appended a `RENDER-BACKLOG.md` recheck reading while at it (still growing, ~97.1h oldest-pending, no material change from this morning's readings — t-044 left untouched, same standing incident already escalated repeatedly). Next preferred lane is roadmap accuracy (lane 2), per the 1->2->3->4 rotation. Rearming to `ready`.

Found and independently verified a real bug in `academy-manager.vue`: the `stylelab` tab renders `<image-upload>` alongside `art-styler` but never called `uploadStore.setTarget()`. `uploadStore.activeTarget` is an app-lifetime Pinia singleton only other pages (`add-bot.vue`, `add-character.vue`, `add-reward.vue`, `add-scenario.vue`, `dream-manager.vue`, `art-maker.vue`) ever write, and none of them clear it on unmount. Two concrete, reachable consequences: (1) opening Academy → Style Lab in a fresh session left `activeTarget` `null`, permanently disabling the upload button ("No upload target configured"); (2) if the user had previously opened e.g. `add-bot.vue` in edit mode (whose target's `applyImage` callback calls `botStore.updateCurrentBot()`), then visited Academy → Style Lab and uploaded an image there, the stale Bot target was still active — silently overwriting that unrelated bot's avatar with the Academy upload. Confirmed `art-styler.vue`'s own upload widget (`sourceTab === 'upload'`) is fully self-contained (`processUploadedFile`, no `uploadStore` involvement), so this only affects the separate sidebar `<image-upload>` widget. Fixed by adding `configureStyleLabUploadTarget()` (mirrors `art-maker.vue`'s existing `configureArtImageUpload()` pattern) and a `watch(activeTab, ..., { immediate: true })` that sets a dedicated `ArtImage`-model target the moment the `stylelab` tab becomes active, before the upload widget is interactive — closing both failure modes. Verified: `npx eslint components/academy/academy-manager.vue` clean, `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`, full project) exit 0, `npx prettier --check` clean. kind_robots PR #1058 (branch `claude/confident-bardeen-jwep28`), all 5 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, verify, facet-catalog, GitGuardian), no review comments — merged squash `7aaa186a`. Next preferred lane is roadmap accuracy (lane 2), per the 1→2→3→4 rotation. Rearmed to `ready`.

Found a real, previously-unfixed gap: `components/art/image-upload.vue` had no `defineEmits()` at all — never emitted `uploaded` at any point in its history (confirmed via `git log -p --all`) — yet `art-maker.vue`'s "Remix Image" disclosure wires `<image-upload @uploaded="handleRemixUploaded" />` and configures the upload purely via `uploadStore.setTarget()` with no `applyImage` callback (the store's only other consumer-notification path, the one `add-bot.vue`/`add-character.vue`/etc. use instead). This meant a user uploading a remix source image never got the "Remix image uploaded. Opening selected image." confirmation and was never switched to the Selected tab, even though the underlying upload itself succeeded — a real, silent dead-code path since the component was written. Fixed by adding a typed `uploaded` emit to `image-upload.vue`, fired with the batch's succeeded ArtImage records once `uploadBatchForActiveTarget()` resolves with at least one success. RAN 2026-07-25 (~15:06-15:33 UTC, claude-conductor-scheduled-20260725T1506Z-t010-lane1, scheduled agent run): option (a) front-end polish (lane 1), per the checklist's next-preferred-lane note (previous cycle ran lane 4, curriculum depth). Dispatched a general-purpose subagent to read every in-scope file with the checklist's exclusion list of every bug class already fixed across PRs #275-#1022. Found a real, previously-unfixed gap in `components/art/art-styler.vue`'s `runStyleTransfer()`: none of the interactive elements that change the current style/source selection (style grid, source-tab switches, gallery/starter selection, upload dropzone, "Clear source" button) were disabled while `isGenerating` was true — only the extra-prompt textarea and the style "Clear" button were. Concrete failure scenario: user selects a style + source, clicks "Apply" (generation starts), then while it's still running picks a different style, a different source image, or clicks "Clear source". When the original, now-abandoned generation resolved, the handler unconditionally wrote `resultImage`/`successMessage` (or `errorMessage` on failure), silently reattaching a stale result/success banner to whatever selection is now on screen, or reviving a preview the user had explicitly cleared. Distinct from every prior source-selection token race fixed in PRs #831/#849/#899 — those guard the *source-selection fetches* against each other; this one is the *generation call itself* racing against later selection changes. Fixed with a new `generationToken` counter following the established `sourceSelectionToken` pattern: captured at the start of `runStyleTransfer()`, bumped by every selection-changing action (`selectStyle`, `clearSelection`, `selectGalleryImage`, `processUploadedFile`, `selectStarterEntry`, `clearSourceImage`, `applySourceImageId`); the success/error handlers only write result/message state if the token is still current when the request settles. The `generated` event still fires unconditionally so downstream consumers (`academyStore.markStyleRemixed` via `academy-remix.vue`) still get credit for a real, saved generation. Verified: `npx eslint`/`npx prettier --check components/art/art-styler.vue` clean; full-project `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) — 0 errors in this file or any Academy surface file (pre-existing unrelated Prisma-schema-mismatch errors elsewhere confirmed present on main before this change, untouched). Sandbox note: `node_modules`/`.nuxt` were regenerated via `npm install`'s postinstall hook this cycle; incidental `package-lock.json` churn reverted before committing so only `art-styler.vue` changed. kind_robots PR #952: all 5 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, facet-catalog, verify, GitGuardian), merged squash `7b0193b`. Added a 30th movement: Joseon Dynasty Korean Genre Painting (`joseon-genre-painting`, curriculum-outline.md §30) — a fourth non-Western entry and the first from Korea, deliberately chosen for visual distinctness from every existing Asian entry already in the curriculum (Song Dynasty's monumental monochrome peaks, Ukiyo-e's flat multi-block color, Mughal's dense courtly ornament): loose, economical brush-line genre scenes of Joseon-era commoners on nearly blank paper, no gold, no dense border, almost no background at all. All three example works confirmed **VERIFIED** directly via `WebFetch` of their live Wikimedia Commons file pages (this session had working egress to `commons.wikimedia.org`): Kim Hong-do's *Ssireum* (Korean Wrestling) and *Seodang* (The Village School), both late 18th century from his *Danwon pungsokdo cheop* album (South Korea's National Treasure 527), rights template `PD-Art (PD-old-100)` plus Creative Commons Public Domain Mark 1.0; and Sin Yun-bok's *Wolha jeongin* (Moonlit Lovers), c. 1805, rights template reading "life plus 70 years or fewer" plus the same PD Mark 1.0. Both artists (Kim Hong-do, 1745–c. 1806; Sin Yun-bok, 1758–after 1813) died over 200 years ago and all three example works predate the 1930 US-publication cutoff by more than a century — clears PUBLIC-DOMAIN-POLICY.md §1.3's both-prongs rule with a wide margin, matching the §22-23/§25-27/§29 direct-file-page-verification precedent rather than a web-search-snippet check. Deliberately selected Kim Hong-do's tamer genre scenes and Sin Yun-bok's most G-rated well-known work (a fully-clothed moonlit courtship scene) over either artist's more explicit "chunhwado"/erotic-genre output, for the Academy's general-audience framing — not a PD-policy requirement, but worth recording since Sin Yun-bok's catalogue spans both registers. Added the placeholder row to `style-lora-registry.md`'s curriculum-slug-mapping table and row 30 to `teaching-notes.md` (mode `prompt`, difficulty Medium — flagged risk: the model likely keeps the source photo's real background under a thin ink-wash filter instead of genuinely emptying the frame, reading as generic "sumi-e" rather than pungsokhwa's actual near-blank-paper look). Added a distinguishing-risk paragraph to curriculum-outline.md's "Lesson-only vs remixable" section contrasting this movement's "empty the frame" risk against `song-dynasty-landscape`'s "discard-the-photo's-composition" risk, since both share the same East Asian ink-and-wash family but fail in opposite directions. Dispatched a general-purpose subagent to read every in-scope file in full (academy-manager.vue, academy-timeline.vue, academy-styles-browser.vue, academy-style-detail.vue, academy-remix.vue, art-styler.vue, image-upload.vue, academyStore.ts, styleHelper.ts, academyStyles.ts) with the checklist's exclusion list of every bug class already fixed across PRs #275-#1022 (focus restoration, aria-* wiring, upload validation/dedup/retry fixes, the sourceSelectionToken/gallerySelectionToken race guards, and every :key remount fix). Found a real, previously-unfixed gap in `image-upload.vue`'s `handleBatchUpload()`: `uploadStore.uploadBatchForActiveTarget()` flips `isUploading` back to `false` the instant the network call settles, but the handler keeps running afterward — holding the per-thumbnail success checkmarks on screen for a `nextTick()` + 700ms pause (the PR #515 fix) and then filtering already-succeeded files out of `queuedFiles`. Since `isUploading` was the only guard on the Upload/Remove/Clear buttons and the function's own re-entry check, a second click on "Upload N images" during that ~700ms+ window slipped past the guard, re-read the still-unfiltered queue (including files the first call had already uploaded), and fired a second upload call — producing duplicate `ArtImage` rows for every already-succeeded file and racing both calls on the same `succeededFiles`/`failedFiles`/`queuedFiles` refs. Fixed by adding a local `isFinalizingUpload` flag held `true` for the whole `handleBatchUpload()` run (network request + checkmark pause + queue filtering, wrapped in try/finally) and gating the guard clause plus all three buttons on `isUploading || isFinalizingUpload`. Verified: `npx prettier --check` clean on the changed file; `npx eslint`/`vue-tsc` could not run in this sandbox (`.nuxt`/`@nuxt/kit` unresolved, no live `DATABASE_URL`) — same known limitation prior lane-1 cycles have hit (e.g. 2026-07-20 12:07 UTC entry below) — relied on kind_robots CI instead. kind_robots PR #947 (branch `claude/dazzling-ptolemy-oobs8y`): all 5 checks green (TypeScript, verify, Contract verifiers, facet-catalog, GitGuardian), merged squash `5d9148a`. Kaizen suggestion (extracting the "hold state visible after an async op settles, guarded against re-entry" pattern into a shared composable) deferred — this is only the second instance of the shape (after PR #515's checkmark pause), not yet worth an abstraction. Dispatched an Explore subagent to read the full 848-line checklist (building the exclusion list of every bug class already fixed across PRs #275-#849) plus every in-scope file in full (art-styler.vue 1620 lines, image-upload.vue 833 lines — actually at components/art/, not components/academy/ — all components/academy/*.vue, academyStore.ts, styleHelper.ts, academyStyles.ts). Found a real, previously-unfixed gap: `applySourceImageId()` — the `sourceImageId`-prop/deep-link path used by `/coloring-page?imageId=...` — was never wired into the `sourceSelectionToken` guard that PR #849 introduced for the other three source-selection paths (upload/starter/gallery). Since `pages/coloring-page.vue` derives the prop reactively from `route.query.imageId` on an instance Vue Router reuses across query-only navigations, a slow deep-link fetch could resolve after the user had already picked a different source (a second deep link, or a manual upload/starter/gallery pick) and silently overwrite it back — a genuine, reachable data-correctness race, not a style nitpick. Fixed by bringing `applySourceImageId()` into the same token guard (capture before the await, bail if stale after) and bumping the token in `clearSourceImage()` too, matching the existing pattern exactly. Verified: `npx eslint`/`npx prettier --check` clean on the changed file; full-project `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) confirmed zero new errors from this change. While polling that PR's CI, caught it failing TypeScript Type Check for a reason unrelated to my own change: main itself (commit 9a5ca202, the merge that landed Build Bench PR #897 plus PR #893) has been red since #897 merged without its own CI having been confirmed green first — `build-bench.vue`'s "Run both" button called a bare `runBoth` that was never defined/destructured locally (only `store.runBoth` exists), and `buildBenchStore.ts`'s `engineDef()` fallback `?? BENCH_ENGINES[0]` didn't type-narrow under this repo's `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`. Fixed both in the same PR (kind_robots PR #899) so CI can go green rather than staying red for whoever pushes next — confirmed via fresh `npx prisma generate` + `vue-tsc --noEmit`: 0 errors after the fix. (The `server/utils/queueControl.ts` errors I initially suspected were part of this were actually a local-sandbox-only artifact of a stale checked-in `prisma/generated/` client predating the `QueueControl` model; a fresh local `prisma generate` resolved them without any source change — not a real CI failure, and not touched.) kind_robots PR #899 merged squash `1322e514`, all 3 CI checks green (API Client Follow-ups Contract, Contract Tests, TypeScript Type Check). Found a real, verifiable gap in `art-styler.vue`'s `selectStarterEntry()` (Starters source-tab picker): `selectedStarterFile.value` was only assigned after the fetch succeeded, in the same synchronous block as `finally { isLoadingStarterImage.value = false }` — with no `await` between them, Vue batches both reactive writes into one render flush, so the loading-spinner branch (gated on `selectedStarterFile === entry.file && isLoadingStarterImage`) never actually painted; a user on a slow connection got no feedback until the checkmark just appeared. Confirmed via `git log`/`git show 8564e9695` this ordering was unchanged since the starters feature shipped (#366) and no later polish PR had touched it. Fixed by setting `selectedStarterFile.value` immediately on click (before the fetch) so the spinner paints during load, with a rollback to `null` in the `catch` block so a failed load doesn't leave a false checkmark. Verified: `npx eslint`/`npx prettier --check` clean on the changed file, full-project `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) exit 0. kind_robots PR #840 (branch `claude/academy-lane1-5b4622`): all 4 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, verify, GitGuardian) — merged squash `cc0b0ebf`. RAN 2026-07-21 (~22:04-22:21 UTC, claude-conductor-scheduled-20260721T220455Z-t010): option (a) front-end polish (lane 1), per the checklist's next-preferred-lane note (previous cycle ran lane 4, curriculum depth). Dispatched an Explore subagent over the full in-scope surface (art-styler.vue, image-upload.vue, all components/academy/*.vue, academyStore.ts, styleHelper.ts, academyStyles.ts) with the checklist's exclusion list of every bug class already fixed in prior cycles. Found a real, verifiable race condition in `art-styler.vue`'s `selectGalleryImage()`: clicking two different not-yet-cached gallery thumbnails in quick succession fires two overlapping `artStore.getArtImageById()` fetches with no way to discard a stale response — whichever resolves last unconditionally wins regardless of click order, silently snapping `selectedSourceImage` back to a no-longer-selected image (and risking the wrong source image being sent to style transfer if "Apply" is hit right after the flicker). Fixed with a simple monotonic `gallerySelectionToken` counter: each call captures the token before its `await` and only commits the `selectedSourceImage.value` write (both success and catch branches) if the token is still current when the promise settles; the `galleryThumbs` cache write still happens unconditionally since caching a stale fetch's thumbnail is harmless. Verified: `npx eslint`/`npx prettier --check` clean, full-project `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) exit 0. kind_robots PR #831 (branch `claude/vigilant-edison-kjvawv`): all 3 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, GitGuardian) — merged squash `5920dbe4`.

Found a real, verifiable rendering bug in `art-styler.vue`'s `sourceImageSrc`/`resultImageSrc` computeds: both treated `img.path` as a URL fallback whenever `img.imagePath` was empty. Traced end-to-end that this is wrong for the Remix Studio's primary flow — `ArtImage.path` is a real URL only for folder-synced collections (`server/utils/syncFolderCollection.ts`, which also sets `imagePath` to the same value); every upload flow instead writes a bracketed metadata tag (`'[UploadedImage]'`, `'[UserAvatar]'`, `'[BotImage]'`, etc. — confirmed all 7 write sites across the app) into `path`, and `imagePath` is only populated outside production (`server/utils/UploadArtImage.ts` line 106). So in production, selecting an uploaded gallery image as a remix source hit `path = '[UploadedImage]'` (truthy) and rendered a broken-image icon in both the "Ready to style" banner and the Source/Result comparison panel, even though a usable `thumbnailData` payload had just been fetched by `selectGalleryImage()` and was sitting unused one branch below. (Noted but did not adopt for this fix: two competing shared resolvers already exist in kind_robots, `utils/artImageSource.ts`'s `isProbablyPath()` and `utils/artImageSrc.ts`'s `resolveArtImageSrc` — the former already handles this exact placeholder-tag case correctly, the latter has the same bug as the pre-fix art-styler.vue code — but neither is used by any Academy/art-styler component today, and the two files also collision-warn on a duplicated `ArtImageLike` export at build time; consolidating art-styler.vue onto one of them is a bigger, separate refactor, filed as a candidate for a future lane-1 or lane-2 cycle rather than bundled into this fix.) Added a small `isPlaceholderImagePath()` guard (bracket-wrapped strings only) so the fallback chain skips a placeholder tag and falls through to `thumbnailData`/`imageData`, leaving folder-synced images (genuine URL in `path`) unaffected. Found a real, verifiable gap: `art-styler.vue`'s `handleFileSelect` (browse-click file-input path) passed any OS-picked file straight to `processUploadedFile()` with zero type validation, unlike its sibling `handleDrop` (drag-and-drop path), which validated via `file.type.startsWith('image/')` — most OS file dialogs let a user switch to "All Files" even with `accept` set, so a non-image selected via browse could silently produce a broken preview or an opaque backend error on style transfer. Extracted a shared `isAcceptedImageFile()` predicate (exact match against `image/png`/`image/jpeg`/`image/webp`) and applied it to both handlers, also tightening `handleDrop`'s looser check (previously let GIF/SVG/BMP/TIFF through silently, contradicting the "PNG · JPEG · WebP" UI copy). RAN 2026-07-20 (~18:12-18:35 UTC, claude-conductor-agent-20260720T1830Z): option (a) front-end polish (lane 1), per the checklist's next-preferred-lane note (previous cycle ran lane 4, curriculum depth). Found a real, verifiable bug in `image-upload.vue`'s `handleBatchUpload()`: on a fully-successful batch upload, the function set the success confirmation banner text and then immediately called `clearQueue()`, which unconditionally resets `message.value`/`error.value` back to `''` on the very next line, in the same synchronous tick — so the "✓ N images uploaded" confirmation never actually rendered on the happy path (the most common outcome). The failure/partial-failure path was unaffected (clearQueue() isn't called there), which is why the asymmetry survived this many polish cycles. Fixed by reordering so clearQueue() runs before the message/error assignment. that already succeeded — `uploadStore.uploadSingleFile` always creates a new

duplicate `ArtImage` rows and duplicate collection/model attachments for the
already-succeeded files. Fixed by splicing succeeded files out of `queuedFiles`
(revoking their object URLs, matching the pattern already used in
`clearQueue`/`removeFile`) as soon as the batch result comes back, so only the
genuinely-failed files remain queued for retry. Verified: `npx eslint`/`npx prettier
--check` on the changed file both clean; `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) exit 0.

Found and independently verified a real, verifiable data-correctness bug: `academy-styles-browser.vue` renders the expanded style's detail panel through a single unkeyed `academy-style-detail` instance; its click handler (`expandedSlug = expandedSlug === style.slug ? null : style.slug`) moves directly between two non-null slugs when the user picks a new grid tile without closing the current panel, so `v-if="expandedStyle"` stays true the whole time and Vue patches the same component instance in place instead of remounting it. `academy-style-detail.vue` records a lesson as viewed only in `onMounted` (`markLessonViewed(props.lesson.slug)`), so switching styles this way silently stopped tracking every style after the first one opened in a session -- no checkmark, no `lessonsViewedCount` credit, no error. Confirmed `academy-timeline.vue` does not share the bug: each style lives in its own `v-for` `<li :key="style.slug">`, so switching styles there always mounts a genuinely distinct instance. Fixed by adding `:key="expandedStyle.slug"` to the `academy-style-detail` usage in `academy-styles-browser.vue`, forcing a remount (and therefore a fresh `onMounted`) on every slug change. Verified: `npx prettier --check` clean; this sandbox has no `node_modules` installed (no local `npm install` run) so eslint/vue-tsc could not run locally -- relying on kind_robots CI (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, GitGuardian) same as the exception path prior cycles have used when local tooling was unavailable. kind_robots PR #646 (branch `claude/keen-fermat-y6jjjw`), all 3 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, GitGuardian), merged squash `98cfdc8`. Found and independently verified a real bug in `art-styler.vue`: `galleryImages` shows up to 48 images in the Remix Studio "Gallery" source tab, but `hydrateGalleryThumbs()` only fetched thumbnail data for the first 24 missing images per call, and was only ever triggered on tab-open or a `gallerySearch` change — any user with more than 24 gallery images saw everything past #24 permanently stuck on the placeholder icon (still selectable, just no thumbnail; the only accidental workaround was narrowing the list via search). The only unpromoted candidate left in `docs/curriculum-candidates/` is `harlem-renaissance.md` — its own text already rules out all four discussed artists (Douglas, Fuller, Motley, Savage) as generation-style/display sources since all died 1958 or later, well past PUBLIC-DOMAIN-POLICY.md's 1956 cutoff. Rather than skip the file, researched a distinct rights-clear option: Winold Reiss (d. 1953), commissioned in 1924 by Survey Graphic to portray Harlem Renaissance figures for its March 1925 "Harlem: Mecca of the New Negro" issue and Alain Locke's "The New Negro" (1925). Reiss clears both PUBLIC-DOMAIN-POLICY.md §1.3 prongs (died 1953; works published 1925). Verified four of his portraits are CC0 on the Smithsonian's own object pages via `WebSearch` (direct `WebFetch` to si.edu/npg.si.edu 403'd this session, same museum-egress limitation the policy doc's own verification log records): W.E.B. Du Bois (NPG.72.79), Roland Hayes (NPG.72.81), James Weldon Johnson (NPG.72.78), Countee Cullen (NPG.98.129.c) — cross-confirmed the Du Bois portrait's dual PD reasoning (1931 publication cutoff + 1953-death life+70) on its Wikimedia Commons file page. Also found two sibling Reiss portraits from the identical 1972 gift (Locke NPG.72.84, Hughes NPG.72.82) marked "Usage conditions apply / © Estate of Winold Reiss" despite an outwardly identical fact pattern — excluded both per PUBLIC-DOMAIN-POLICY.md §5's default-deny-under-ambiguity rule, used only the four confirmed-CC0 works. Did NOT promote the candidate into `curriculum-outline.md` this cycle: Reiss was not a Harlem Renaissance movement artist himself (a white, German-American illustrator commissioned to portray it), which is a materially different situation from every prior "one artist clears the cutoff" promotion (Precisionism §33, American Regionalism §24), and the candidate's own promotion checklist requires "a Black cultural-history reviewer checks the framing" before promotion — a call outside what this pass should make unilaterally. Documented the full research (with rights metadata per PUBLIC-DOMAIN-POLICY.md §3) in the candidate file's new "Research update" section, checked off the two rights-related checklist items, and left the reviewer item explicitly open with the reasoning. RAN 2026-07-27 (claude-scheduled-20260727T071500Z-aa-t010-lane1): option (a), front-end polish. Dispatched an Explore agent over all 5 Academy components, academyStore.ts, academyStyles.ts, stores/helpers/styleHelper.ts, art-styler.vue, and image-upload.vue with the full exclusion list of previously-fixed bug classes (PRs #275-#622, #1000, #1015, #1017, #1027, #1037). Found and independently verified a real bug in `academyStore.ts`: `academyStylesBySlug` (`stores/seeds/academyStyles.ts`) was built with `Object.fromEntries(...)`, a plain object with the normal `Object.prototype` chain, and every "is this a real curriculum slug" check in the store (`hydrate()`, `selectStyle()`, `markLessonViewed()`, `markStyleRemixed()`) used the `in` operator against it -- which walks the prototype chain, so inherited keys like "constructor"/"toString"/"valueOf" evaluate as present even though no curriculum entry has that slug. Concretely: `hydrate()` reads client-writable `localStorage['kindRobotsAcademyState']`; a value like `{"selectedStyleSlug":"constructor"}` would pass the `in` check, resolve `selectedStyle` to the real JS `Object` constructor function (truthy, not `undefined`), and crash `academy-style-detail.vue` reading `lesson.remix.template` on it. Verified the mechanism with a standalone Node repro before treating it as real. Fixed at the one definition site -- gave `academyStylesBySlug` a `null` prototype via `Object.assign(Object.create(null), Object.fromEntries(...))` -- which closes all 7 call sites in `academyStore.ts` at once rather than patching each check individually. Blast radius today is narrower than a bug reachable through ordinary clicking (only entry point is a corrupted/hand-crafted localStorage value, no current in-app path passes unsanitized strings into the mutators), but it's a genuine defect in the store's own trust boundary for persisted state, not a cosmetic nitpick. Verified: `source scripts/provision_kind_robots_deps.sh` + `npm run test` (`vue-tsc --noEmit`) clean, `npx eslint stores/seeds/academyStyles.ts` clean. kind_robots PR #1040 (branch `claude/confident-bardeen-98gjsk`), all 5 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, facet-catalog, verify, GitGuardian), no review comments -- merged squash `cef2175`. Next preferred lane is roadmap accuracy (lane 2), per the 1->2->3->4 rotation. Rearmed to ready.

RAN 2026-07-27T09:19:08Z (claude-scheduled-20260727T081200Z-aa-t010-lane3): option (c), inspiration assets. `python scripts/recheck_render_queue.py` read `healthy: queueDepth: DONE=1920, FAILED=5, CANCELLED=771 (all-time). oldestPending: none` -- the first `healthy`/zero-pending reading of the day after a sustained `growing` backlog (PENDING=144, oldest job ~61h) logged in every earlier check today. Treated this as a genuine window per the checklist's blocker-discipline rule (recheck only on a material state change) and re-probed live rather than falling back to lane 4 again.

Before queuing anything, investigated why `art-prompts.yaml` had zero `kind-robots-academy-style-preview-*` entries left despite the checklist claiming "33 prompts queued... all still status: pending" as of a recent cycle, and despite no academy images existing anywhere in the kind_robots checkout (`stores/seeds/academyStyles.ts` has no `previewImageSrc` populated on any of its 30 styles, confirmed via grep; `find ... -path "*academy*"` under kind_robots' `public/images` returned nothing). Root cause, confirmed by reading `scripts/distribute_images.py`'s `distribute()` and `prune_art_prompts()`: when a `requests:` entry's `target_repo` is `silasfelinus/kind_robots` and a local kind_robots checkout happens to be present (true in most agent sandboxes per AGENTS.md's cross-repo verification setup), the script copies the file straight into that checkout's `public/images/...` and treats it as "moved" -- but kind_robots' `.gitignore` line 33 is `/public/images/**`, so nothing about that copy is ever committed, pushed, or deployed. `distribute()` then feeds the same filename into `prune_art_prompts()`, which deletes the now-"delivered" request from `art-prompts.yaml` entirely. Net effect: the image gets generated, copied to a local dead end, and the only record that it ever needed real delivery vanishes -- exactly the failure signature that explains the 33 missing academy-style-preview entries (and, structurally, would silently eat any other kind_robots-targeted request processed the same way, e.g. inspirations). This was NOT a new phenomenon this cycle -- it's a pre-existing bug that just happened to be root-caused now while investigating an unrelated discrepancy.

Confirmed the intended architecture already exists elsewhere but wasn't wired into the script: `.github/workflows/distribute-images.yml`'s own header comment says "Kind Robots-targeted ArtJobs now use the home relay's direct self-hosted media path... This workflow intentionally does NOT check out kind_robots or commit public/images binaries," and its final step explicitly warns and leaves such files in `projects/process/` rather than delivering them via git. The CI workflow only avoided the bug by accident (no kind_robots checkout exists in that runner, so the script's `KIND_ROBOTS_ROOT.exists()` guard happened to skip it there) -- any session with a local kind_robots checkout, which is the norm per this repo's own cross-repo verification guidance, hit the bug.

Fix: `scripts/distribute_images.py` -- changed the kind_robots branch in `distribute()` to unconditionally retain the file in `projects/process/` (never copy into `KIND_ROBOTS_ROOT`, regardless of whether it exists locally), removed the now-dead `kr_moved`/"git add public/" guidance from the final "Next steps" print (that instruction was already wrong post-relay-migration since `public/images/**` is git-ignored), added a "Retained (need kind_robots relay/media-path delivery, not git)" summary list, and updated the module docstring to document the new invariant. Added `tests/test_distribute_images.py::test_kind_robots_target_retained_not_pruned`, which builds a fake kind_robots checkout + a kind_robots-targeted `requests:` entry and asserts the file stays in `projects/process/`, is never copied into the checkout, and the request entry is never pruned. Full suite: `python -m pytest tests/` -- 623 passed, 1 skipped (pre-existing skip, unrelated), no regressions.

Verified the fix against a real live request rather than only a synthetic test: added `kind-robots-academy-style-preview-greek-vase-painting` to `art-prompts.yaml` (256x256, prompt adapted from `curriculum-outline.md` §1's Ancient Greek Vase Painting `remix_hint`, a PD-safe ancient-pottery subject), dry-ran then live-ran `consume_art_requests.py --id-prefix "kind-robots-academy-style-preview-" --live --limit 1` -- job 2697 rendered successfully (ArtImage 12885) within the timeout, confirming the relay really is healthy and not just between-poll noise. The script downloaded a PNG (Pillow wasn't installed in this sandbox, so it fell back to PNG instead of webp) -- installed Pillow, converted to webp, then ran the now-fixed `distribute_images.py`: it correctly retained the file in `projects/process/` instead of writing it into the local kind_robots checkout (verified `git status` in kind_robots stayed clean throughout). Removed a stray pre-fix local copy that a first (pre-fix) distribute run had already written into the kind_robots checkout's git-ignored `public/images/academy/styles/` before the fix landed, so no misleading local state remains for a future session to trip over. Re-added the `art-prompts.yaml` request entry with `status: done` and a `note:` explaining what's generated (ArtJob 2697 / ArtImage 12885) versus what's still pending (relay/media-path delivery to kind_robots) -- this is now the durable, visible record that a future cycle or Silas can act on, replacing the silent-disappearance behavior the bug used to produce.

Deliberately scoped this cycle to proving-and-fixing the pipeline rather than bulk-regenerating all ~29 remaining movement previews in one pass -- `style-remix-configs.yaml` and `curriculum-outline.md`'s per-style `remix_hint` fields already have enough raw material to derive standalone preview prompts for most of them, so a future lane-3 cycle can batch-generate through the now-safe pipeline without repeating this investigation. t-019 (wire `previewImageSrc` once real files land) remains correctly blocked -- no static file actually reached kind_robots' deployed path this cycle, delivery still requires relay/media-server write access this sandbox doesn't hold (same category of gap as the `media.acrocatranch.com` manifest blocker documented for t-033/t-034).

Found a real, previously-unfixed bug in the New/Explored progress filter PR #1068 had just added: `academy-styles-browser.vue`'s grid (`filteredStyles`) and its detail panel (`expandedStyle`) read progress from two independent sources, and `academy-style-detail.vue` marks a lesson viewed as soon as it mounts (`allowMarkViewed` defaults to `true`, never overridden here) -- so under the "New" filter, opening a still-unviewed lesson caused its own tile to vanish from the grid on the next reactive tick while its detail panel was still open, and the grid reflowed around the gap. This also silently broke `closeStyle()`'s focus restoration: `gridRefs.get(slug)` was already `undefined` once the tile unmounted (the `:ref` callback fires with `null` on removal), so focus fell back to the search input instead of the button the user actually clicked. Fixed by keeping the currently-expanded style's tile in `filteredStyles` regardless of the progress filter -- it now only leaves the "New" list once the user closes it, matching what they'd actually expect, and `closeStyle()`'s focus restoration works correctly again. Verified: `npx vue-tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx prettier --check` clean (reverted one unrelated pre-existing formatting drift `--write` also touched, to keep the diff to the intended single line). kind_robots PR #1071 (branch `claude/confident-bardeen-6d1bxn`), all 5 CI checks green (TypeScript, Contract verifiers, facet-catalog, verify, GitGuardian), no review comments -- merged squash `a1f130a`. RAN 2026-07-28T10:12:00Z (claude-conductor-scheduled-20260728T100512Z-aa-t010-lane3): option (c), inspiration assets. Added `docs/inspiration-sets/mountain-gate-four-traditions.md`, a controlled comparison teaching sequence spanning four courtly/scholarly painting traditions that never overlap with any prior set in this collection: Persian Miniature (Timurid/Safavid, c. 1400-1600), Mughal Miniature (Imperial atelier, c. 1560-1650), Song Dynasty Landscape (Northern Song, c. 950-1130), and Joseon Dynasty Korean Genre Painting (c. 1750-1820) -- extending the diversity direction flower-arranger established (Byzantine/Ukiyo-e/Art Nouveau/Vienna Secession) further outside the Western canon; three of the four traditions here are non-Western and the fourth predates the Western canon entirely. The fixed composition (a traveler buying fruit at a garden gate, with a distant mountain beyond a wall) was chosen specifically so the four traditions' wildly different treatment of "background" is the actual comparison: Persian miniature keeps the mountain as flat ornament placed higher-not-smaller, Mughal miniature lets it recede atmospherically, Song Dynasty landscape inflates it into the dominant subject (shrinking both figures to small legible details), and Joseon genre painting drops it almost entirely in favor of bare-paper economy. Followed the flower-arranger/garden-gate section structure (Recognition cues / Remix prompt / Negative guidance / Common failure per entry, plus Rights boundary, Blind comparison exercise, Reflection prompts, Reproducibility record, Promotion gate). Cross-referenced `curriculum-outline.md` sections 25/26/27/30 for each tradition's recognition cues and notable-workshop attribution so remix prompts stay movement-level (no named artists) and historically grounded. Rights boundary section explicitly instructs against collapsing the four traditions into a generic "Asian"/"Islamic" label, naming each tradition's specific court/workshop and geography, matching the specificity precedent flower-arranger set for its Ukiyo-e entry. Prompt-first planning artifact -- no live generation attempted or required this cycle, consistent with how every prior inspiration-assets lane (everyday-modernity, one-room) shipped as doc-only teaching sequences. Verified: `python scripts/validate_roadmaps.py` and `python scripts/audit_roadmaps.py` both clean before opening the PR. Conductor-only change (kind_robots untouched). Next preferred lane is curriculum depth (lane 4), per the 1->2->3->4 rotation. Rearming to ready.

RAN 2026-07-29T06:10:00Z (conductor scheduled cycle, session claude-conductor-scheduled-20260729-aa-t010-lane3): option (c), inspiration/preview assets, continuing from the 2026-07-29T04:20Z stranded-state correction that reset `next_lane` back to 3. Root-caused why the 2026-07-28 fauvism-thumbnail generation never landed rather than just re-attempting the render blind: `projects/process/fauvism.webp` (ArtJob 2775 / ArtImage 13125) was still sitting on disk, untouched since 2026-07-28, but its `art-prompts.yaml` `requests:` entry was gone and the file had been swept into `projects/process/unmatched/` by the most recent `distribute_images.py` run (`git log` on `projects/art-prompts.yaml`/`projects/process/` pinned the actual removal to commit `97f90d8`, "chore: distribute images from projects/process", one run before the "unmatched" move in `a84595c`).

Traced the mechanism: `distribute_images.py`'s `prune_art_prompts()` delegates completeness to `build_workspace.py`'s `request_is_complete()`, which returns `True` the instant a request's `status` field reads `done` — with no distinction between "generation succeeded" (what `consume_art_requests.py`'s `mark_done()` actually records, immediately after rendering) and "delivery to the kind_robots media host confirmed" (a separate step: the home relay writes directly during generation, or `distribute_images.py` RETAINs a local fallback copy in `projects/process/` when relay delivery didn't happen inline). Any `distribute_images.py` run where *any other* file successfully moves triggers this prune, so a retained-but-undelivered kind_robots request gets silently dropped the moment its status flips to done, regardless of whether delivery ever completed — a second, independent trigger for the same class of bug the 2026-07-27 fix (never writing a local kind_robots checkout copy) only partially closed. Confirmed via `curl -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}'` that `https://media.acrocatranch.com/images/academy/styles/fauvism.webp` is still 404, so the render genuinely never reached production — this wasn't a case of the tracking data merely lagging a real delivery.

Fixed `request_is_complete()` in `scripts/build_workspace.py`: for `target_repo: silasfelinus/kind_robots` requests, `status: done` is no longer sufficient on its own — it also requires the request's staged file (`consume_art_queue_core.staged_filename()`) to no longer exist in `projects/process/`. Added `tests/test_build_workspace.py` (4 cases: staged-file-present keeps it pending, staged-file-removed completes it, the slug/variant staged-name path, and a control case confirming non-kind_robots requests are unaffected). Moved `fauvism.webp` back from `projects/process/unmatched/` into `projects/process/` and restored its `art-prompts.yaml` request entry (same content as the pruned original, plus a note on what happened and why); also removed the stray `italian-futurism.webp` duplicate found alongside it in `unmatched/` — confirmed via the same curl check that `italian-futurism.webp` is already live (200) at the production media host with no yaml record, so that copy was a harmless leftover duplicate, safe to delete rather than restore.

Verified: `python -m pytest tests/` (761 passed, 1 skipped, 30 subtests, includes the 4 new cases), `python scripts/distribute_images.py --dry-run` now prints `RETAIN (needs relay/media-path delivery) fauvism.webp` instead of routing it to unmatched (`0 unmatched` in the run), `python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(...)"` on `art-prompts.yaml` parses clean with no duplicate request ids, `python scripts/validate_roadmaps.py` valid, `python scripts/audit_roadmaps.py` clean (0 errors, same 6 pre-existing warnings). Did not attempt to re-run the actual render or relay delivery this cycle — the existing local render already passed visual inspection last cycle, and there's no in-repo mechanism to retry only the delivery half for an already-generated file without discarding it and re-rendering from scratch (out of scope for this fix). Next cycle with home-relay access should re-verify `media.acrocatranch.com/.../fauvism.webp` and, if still 404, work out how delivery is meant to be retried for an already-staged kind_robots render. Conductor-only PR. Next preferred lane is curriculum depth (lane 4), per the 1→2→3→4 rotation. Rearmed to `ready`.

Found a real, previously-unfixed bug in `components/art/image-upload.vue`: `clearQueue()` unconditionally called `clearModelSelection()` (`botStore.deselectBot()`, `characterStore.deselectCharacter()`, `dreamStore.deselectDream()`, `rewardStore.deselectReward()`, `scenarioStore.deselectScenario()`), even though the model-connect UI and its `connectedModelType` select only render when `showModelConnect` is true -- every real call site in the app (Academy Style Lab, `art-maker`, `art-builder`, `add-bot`/`-character`/`-reward`/`-scenario`, `avatar-picker`, ...) passes `showModelConnect` false/default, so a visitor never touches `connectedModelType`, yet `handleBatchUpload()` calls `clearQueue()` automatically after every fully-successful upload. Concrete failure: a user mid-edit on `/add-bot?id=7` (`currentBot` set, persisted to `localStorage` via `botStore.persist()`) navigates to Academy Style Lab, uploads images purely for the gallery, and the successful upload silently nulls their in-progress Bot selection; same for Character/Dream/Reward/Scenario, and `scenarioStore.deselectScenario()` additionally clears a shared sheet. Same bug class already fixed for `uploadStore.activeTarget` (PRs #1058, #1063, #1067, #1162), just via a different store family this time -- independently verified the claim myself (read `deselectBot`/`deselectScenario`, confirmed `persist()`/`syncToLocalStorage()`/`clearSheet` calls, traced `handleBatchUpload`'s automatic `clearQueue()` call on a clean batch) before implementing, rather than taking the agent's report at face value. Fixed by gating the `clearModelSelection()` call in `clearQueue()` on `props.showModelConnect`, matching the two other call sites (the select's `@change`, the "Unlink" button), which already only render inside `v-if="showModelConnect"`. Verified: `vue-tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx eslint components/art/image-upload.vue` clean, `npx prettier --check` clean (ran `provision_kind_robots_deps.sh` first). kind_robots PR #1466 (branch `worker/ai-art-academy-t010-upload-model-leak`), all 14 CI checks green, squash-merged `d1f34794`. `python scripts/validate_roadmaps.py` clean before merge.
